# claude-plugins-community — community-submitted plugin marketplace (snapshot 2026-05-21)


**Total plugins:** 1715
**Source of truth:** `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` in [anthropics/claude-plugins-community](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-community)
**Note:** Read-only mirror of the directory at [clau.de/plugin-directory-submission](https://clau.de/plugin-directory-submission). Anthropic does not maintain the plugins themselves — each plugin lives in its submitter's own repo. Anthropic owns this catalog (`owner.name: Anthropic`), publishes the validated `marketplace.json`, and runs validate/scan/bump CI workflows. Submissions arrive via the directory form and land here through periodic bulk sync commits.

## Schema

Each entry has `name` + `description` + `source` (either `{source: 'url', url, sha}` for whole-repo refs or `{source: 'git-subdir', url, path, sha}` for monorepo sub-paths). Pinned SHAs are bumped by a `bump-plugin-shas` CI job. Only 41 of 1715 entries (2.4%) carry an upstream `category` field, so the categorization below is heuristic over name+description.
